/* =========================================================

   Delete this whole block if the treatment is rejected.

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   CARD SURFACE
--------------------------------------------------------- */

.plant-card-button {
  position: relative;

  display: flex;
  align-items: stretch;
  gap: 12px;

  width: 100%;
  min-width: 0;

  margin: 0;
  padding: 14px 15px;

  border: 1px solid rgba(72, 92, 67, 0.14);
  border-radius: 17px;

  background:
    linear-gradient(
      145deg,
      rgba(255, 254, 249, 0.98),
      rgba(250, 247, 237, 0.94)
    );

  color: #344c39;
  text-align: left;

  box-shadow:
    0 5px 14px rgba(48, 74, 50, 0.055);

  cursor: pointer;

  transition:
    transform 150ms ease,
    border-color 150ms ease,
    background 150ms ease,
    box-shadow 150ms ease;
}

  transform: translateY(-1px);

      145deg,

      #fbf8ef
    );

    0 9px 20px rgba(48, 74, 50, 0.085);
}

  outline-offset: 2px;
}

   MAIN CONTENT

}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   RICH PLANTS-PAGE DETAILS

   These should read as useful story context, not as
   miniature grey dashboard boxes.

  display: grid;

/* =======================================
   SPRIG FORMS
   FUNCTIONAL DEVELOPMENT LAYOUT

   Deliberately simple while Sprig's
   architecture is being built.

/* =======================================
   GLOBAL SPRIG SAVE GUARD

   Sprig's database service adds
   .sprig-is-saving synchronously whenever
   GardenData is being written.

   Main save controls and quick-add save
   controls therefore cannot receive a
   second tap while that write is underway.

   This is deliberately global so nested
   Ingredient, Product and Recipe controls
   receive the same protection as the
   large Plant / Journal / Harvest forms.
======================================= */

.sprig-is-saving .enter-button,
.sprig-is-saving .sprig-picker-custom-save {
  pointer-events:
    none;

  opacity:
    0.62;

  cursor:
    wait;
}


/*
 * Some editors use ordinary submit buttons
 * without Sprig's main enter-button class.
 *
 * Catch those too.
 */

.sprig-is-saving
form
button[type="submit"] {
  pointer-events:
    none;

  opacity:
    0.62;

  cursor:
    wait;
}

   not recreate the page shell,
   navigation, header or Back to top.

   FORMS OPEN
======================================= */

/*
 * Sprig forms are modal working pages.
 *
 * While one is open, hide the persistent
 * bottom navigation so it cannot overlap
 * the form, obscure save controls or allow
 * accidental navigation away mid-entry.
 */

 .bottom-navigation {
   opacity: 0;
   visibility: hidden;
   pointer-events: none;
 }

/* =======================================
   GARDEN OF MINE
   UNIFORM INTERACTIVE CONTROLS

   One visual language for everyday
   buttons, form fields, selectors,
   segmented choices and filter controls.

   Page CSS may control layout.
   This file controls appearance.
======================================= */


/* =======================================
   SHARED CONTROL TOKENS
======================================= */

:root {
    --gom-control-bg: rgba(255, 253, 247, 0.96);
    --gom-control-bg-hover: rgba(255, 255, 251, 0.99);
    --gom-control-bg-selected: #e4eddc;
  
    --gom-control-border: rgba(65, 88, 58, 0.18);
    --gom-control-border-hover: rgba(65, 88, 58, 0.32);
    --gom-control-border-selected: rgba(63, 93, 57, 0.48);
  
    --gom-control-text: #304a32;
    --gom-control-muted: #687267;
  
    --gom-primary: #527047;
    --gom-primary-hover: #45643f;
    --gom-primary-deep: #3f5d3e;
  
    --gom-control-radius: 14px;
    --gom-pill-radius: 999px;
  
    --gom-control-shadow:
      0 4px 12px rgba(48, 74, 50, 0.055);
  
    --gom-control-shadow-hover:
      0 7px 18px rgba(48, 74, 50, 0.09);
  
    --gom-focus-ring:
      0 0 0 3px rgba(82, 112, 71, 0.13);
  }
  
  
  /* =======================================
     REMOVE NATIVE CONTROL PERSONALITY
  
     Browsers should not decide what
     Garden of Mine looks like.
  ======================================= */
  
  button,
  input,
  select,
  textarea {
    font: inherit;
  }
  
  button,
  select {
    color: var(--gom-control-text);
  }
  
  input,
  select,
  textarea {
    accent-color: var(--gom-primary);
  }
  
  
  /* =======================================
     TEXT / SEARCH / NUMBER / DATE FIELDS
  
     Deliberately broad inside forms so
     Add Plant, Journal, Harvest, Growing
     Places, Recipes, Products etc all
     inherit the same field language.
  ======================================= */
  
  form
  input:not([type='checkbox']):not([type='radio']):not([type='file']):not([type='range']):not([type='color']),
  form
  select,
  form
  textarea,
  .sprig-paper-panel
  input:not([type='checkbox']):not([type='radio']):not([type='file']):not([type='range']):not([type='color']),
  .sprig-paper-panel
  select,
  .sprig-paper-panel
  textarea {
    box-sizing: border-box;
  
    width: 100%;
    min-width: 0;
  
    margin: 0;
  
    border:
      1px solid
      var(--gom-control-border);
  
    border-radius:
      var(--gom-control-radius);
  
    outline: none;
  
    background:
      var(--gom-control-bg);
  
    color:
      var(--gom-control-text);
  
    font: inherit;
  
    line-height: 1.35;
  
    box-shadow:
      inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.72),
      var(--gom-control-shadow);
  
    transition:
      border-color 150ms ease,
      background 150ms ease,
      box-shadow 150ms ease,
      transform 150ms ease;
  }
  
  
  form
  input:not([type='checkbox']):not([type='radio']):not([type='file']):not([type='range']):not([type='color']),
  form
  select,
  .sprig-paper-panel
  input:not([type='checkbox']):not([type='radio']):not([type='file']):not([type='range']):not([type='color']),
  .sprig-paper-panel
  select {
    min-height: 46px;
  
    padding:
      10px
      13px;
  }
  
  
  form
  textarea,
  .sprig-paper-panel
  textarea {
    min-height: 108px;
  
    padding:
      12px
      13px;
  
    resize: vertical;
  }
  
  
  form
  input:not([type='checkbox']):not([type='radio']):not([type='file']):not([type='range']):not([type='color']):hover,
  form
  select:hover,
  form
  textarea:hover,
  .sprig-paper-panel
  input:not([type='checkbox']):not([type='radio']):not([type='file']):not([type='range']):not([type='color']):hover,
  .sprig-paper-panel
  select:hover,
  .sprig-paper-panel
  textarea:hover {
    border-color:
      var(--gom-control-border-hover);
  
    background:
      var(--gom-control-bg-hover);
  }
  
  
  form
  input:not([type='checkbox']):not([type='radio']):not([type='file']):not([type='range']):not([type='color']):focus,
  form
  select:focus,
  form
  textarea:focus,
  .sprig-paper-panel
  input:not([type='checkbox']):not([type='radio']):not([type='file']):not([type='range']):not([type='color']):focus,
  .sprig-paper-panel
  select:focus,
  .sprig-paper-panel
  textarea:focus {
    border-color:
      rgba(63, 93, 57, 0.48);
  
    background:
      #fffef9;
  
    box-shadow:
      var(--gom-focus-ring);
  }
  
  
  form
  input::placeholder,
  form
  textarea::placeholder,
  .sprig-paper-panel
  input::placeholder,
  .sprig-paper-panel
  textarea::placeholder {
    color:
      rgba(70, 84, 67, 0.56);
  
    opacity: 1;
  }
  
  
  /* =======================================
     SELECTS
  
     Give the CLOSED control Garden of Mine
     styling rather than browser styling.
  ======================================= */
  
  form select,
  .sprig-paper-panel select,
  .plant-browser-sort select,
  .growing-sort-field select {
    appearance: none;
    -webkit-appearance: none;
  
    padding-right: 42px;
  
    cursor: pointer;
  
    background-color:
      var(--gom-control-bg);
  
    background-image:
      linear-gradient(
        45deg,
        transparent 50%,
        #52694d 50%
      ),
      linear-gradient(
        135deg,
        #52694d 50%,
        transparent 50%
      );
  
    background-position:
      calc(100% - 18px) 50%,
      calc(100% - 13px) 50%;
  
    background-size:
      5px 5px,
      5px 5px;
  
    background-repeat:
      no-repeat;
  }
  
  
  form select:focus,
  .sprig-paper-panel select:focus,
  .plant-browser-sort select:focus,
  .growing-sort-field select:focus {
    background-color:
      #fffef9;
  }
  
  
  /*
   * Browser / OS popup menus have limited
   * styling support, but use Garden colours
   * wherever the platform permits it.
   */
  
  select option {
    background:
      #fffdf7;
  
    color:
      #304a32;
  }
  
  
  /* =======================================
     CHECKBOXES + RADIO BUTTONS
  
     This kills the giant blue browser
     targets without requiring markup
     changes throughout the app.
  ======================================= */
  
  form
  input[type='checkbox'],
  form
  input[type='radio'],
  .sprig-paper-panel
  input[type='checkbox'],
  .sprig-paper-panel
  input[type='radio'] {
    appearance: none;
    -webkit-appearance: none;
  
    position: relative;
  
    flex:
      0 0 auto;
  
    width:
      19px;
  
    height:
      19px;
  
    min-width:
      19px;
  
    min-height:
      19px;
  
    margin:
      1px
      0;
  
    padding:
      0;
  
    border:
      1.5px solid
      rgba(65, 88, 58, 0.38);
  
    background:
      rgba(255, 253, 247, 0.98);
  
    cursor:
      pointer;
  
    box-shadow:
      inset 0 1px 1px rgba(48, 74, 50, 0.04);
  
    transition:
      border-color 140ms ease,
      background 140ms ease,
      box-shadow 140ms ease,
      transform 140ms ease;
  }
  
  
  form
  input[type='radio'],
  .sprig-paper-panel
  input[type='radio'] {
    border-radius:
      50%;
  }
  
  
  form
  input[type='checkbox'],
  .sprig-paper-panel
  input[type='checkbox'] {
    border-radius:
      6px;
  }
  
  
  form
  input[type='radio']:checked,
  .sprig-paper-panel
  input[type='radio']:checked {
    border-color:
      var(--gom-primary);
  
    background:
      radial-gradient(
        circle,
        var(--gom-primary) 0 4px,
        #fffdf7 5px 100%
      );

/* =======================================
   SPRIG GLOBAL PRINT FOUNDATION
======================================= */

/*
 * This stylesheet owns Sprig-wide print
 * behaviour.
 *
 * Feature stylesheets may control how
 * their own reports look, but they must
 * not globally hide or reshape unrelated
 * Sprig pages.
 *
 * Architecture:
 *
 *   sprig-print.css
 *     Global print behaviour and shared
 *     print foundations.
 *
 *   Feature CSS
 *     The appearance of that feature's
 *     own printed content.
 *
 * This prevents one report type from
 * accidentally affecting another.
 */


/* =======================================
   PRINT TARGETS
======================================= */

/*
 * Some Sprig exports use a dedicated
 * print-only report in the existing DOM.
 *
 * When one of those reports is present,
 * Sprig isolates that report for printing.
 *
 * Ordinary printable pages, including
 * Sprig Smart, do not use these selectors
 * and therefore remain untouched.
 */

 @media print {

    /* =====================================
       COMPARISON REPORT
    ===================================== */
  
    body:has(.sprig-comparison-report) * {
      visibility:
        hidden;
    }
  
    body:has(.sprig-comparison-report)
    .sprig-comparison-report,
    body:has(.sprig-comparison-report)
    .sprig-comparison-report * {
      visibility:
        visible;
    }
  
  
    /* =====================================
       GARDEN TRIAL REPORT
    ===================================== */
  
    body:has(.sprig-trial-print-report) * {
      visibility:
        hidden !important;
    }
  
    body:has(.sprig-trial-print-report)
    .sprig-trial-print-report,
    body:has(.sprig-trial-print-report)
    .sprig-trial-print-report * {
      visibility:
        visible !important;
    }
  }
